# Gig: Olive Branch

Journal: `quests/street_stories/wesbrook/japan_town/sts_wbr_jpn_01` · `StreetStory` · hash `2269454619`.

Journal premise: Gig type: Special Delivery\nObjective: Meet with Sergei Karasinsky and deliver his gift to the designated address\nLocation: Garage on Redwood St.\nDetails:\n\nSergei Karasinsky called. He's a solo who, like my husband, acts before he thinks. It's a personal characteristic that always leads to the same thing sooner or later – trouble. Fortunately for us, Sergei is not only a man who acts without thinking but a panicked one at that. This means the pay is good and almost literally flying out of his account into ours.\n\nSergei is waiting for you at Silk Road. You will meet him there and pick up the peace offering he would like delivered to the Tyger Claws. He can't do this himself because he's recently been added to their blacklist.\n\nThe boy believes this small token will help the Tygers find forgiveness in their hearts for his terrible mistake.

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 147 exact journal-linked nodes in 6 resources; 14 resources in the inspected dependency context.

Resource roles in that context: 12 content candidate, 2 repair. Role labels describe path/name evidence, not proof of runtime reachability.

World NodeRef definitions/streaming owners, full actor and animation closure, numeric TweakDB names, spoken subtitle/VO payload joins and installed/save behavior have not been validated by this structural pass. Raw direct references, actor bindings, choices, event locstrings and community entries remain in the evidence shards.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

551 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 54 distinct direct resource identities; 31 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.
